# Graph Report - hackathon  (2026-11-14)

## Corpus Check
- 3478 files · ~5,446,156 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 550 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .log 241, .csv 180, .jsonl 94)

## Summary
- 86181 nodes · 186962 edges · 2997 communities (2042 shown, 955 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 87% EXTRACTED · 13% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 24230 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output
